  has its own explicit value/unit. Transfers/previews/load never generate production income twice.

  income/budget reservations and advanced Tycoon provider execution remain open. Recovery-cost
  additions compile but remain unexecuted; do not claim their runtime proof.

  binds no IEconomy/IBusinessAccounts. Scenario accounts settings select an account owner;
  income_policy omission/null means no generation settlement. Income policy selection requires
  accounts and actual resource transactions. Monetary construction requires explicitly authored
  costs, never the mere presence of values/accounts. No survival buyer/seller/money requirement.

- Read `plans/enhancements/MASTER_TRACKER.md` and the relevant stage before architecture changes.
- Runtime: `src/Beep.SimEngine`, assembly/namespace `Beep.SimEngine`, `netstandard2.1`.
  No dependency on OilFieldDays, clients, test infrastructure, renderer or platform SDK.
- OilFieldDays is a normal extension. No game assembly gets `InternalsVisibleTo`. Framework tests
  may instrument internals; `ExtensionProof` must compile using public contracts only.
- Initialize/Update has one engine owner. Game.Update calls each live entity.Update in the declared
  game-update phase; the entity alone commits its resolved state. Draw/rendering belongs to a future game
  platform, is not a SimEngine lifecycle phase and is not part of this implementation.
- Extend existing registries, job/reservation stores, state codecs and view projections, never
  parallel authorities. Capability parts are not entities. No legacy loaders or compatibility facades.
- Authored content is JSON through the master manifest; executable behavior implements registered
  contracts. Missing/invalid definitions fail explicitly. Do not fabricate default gameplay.
- ContentAccessModule selects exactly one frozen actual IContentAccessDefinition; no base factory
  or detached stateful basis. ContentAccessPolicy alone aggregates seat/visibility, basis and sorted
  declared IContentAccessRule restrictions. Mutable rules use existing service codec ownership.
  Rule identity/session and callbacks share original provider/clock/identity/map observation;
  recursive or swallowed-recursive checks latch the original fault. No host access policy/cache.
- IPhysicalWorkEffect callbacks share PhysicalWorkEffectContracts for actual order/binding/subject,
  positive machine ancestry and earned completion. Direct dispatch definitions share strict
  TransformationDispatchDeclaration and the existing queue owner. Pure subject callbacks and refused
  commits guard actual Entity.Sync; Applied effects cannot change clock time/pause/speed.
  WorkEffectDef/PhysicalWorkEffectDef are deleted. Current definitions implement the contracts
  directly; condition completion shares one JSON declaration and the original condition owner.
  Resolver observation includes the existing CompletedBy worker's actual Entity.Sync, not a
  second subject/worker ledger. Remaining domain/provider runtime breadth is explicitly open.
- KnowledgeVisibilityModule selects IKnowledgeVisibilityDefinition, policy rules resolve IVisibilityTarget,
  and definition/regional knowledge targets resolve IKnowledgeSubjectDefinition. VisibilityTargetDef is
  deleted; sole strict policy/target declarations drive native and independent registered definitions.
  Original KnowledgeVisibility owns actual policy evaluation, guarded callbacks and transient reentry
  refusal; CompanyKnowledgeService remains sole history/freshness owner. Queried entity callbacks
  observe that entity's actual Sync. Scoped observation is not global private-state rollback/sandbox.
- Authored knowledge subjects and sharing/forgetting policies select actual frozen contract aliases;
  obsolete KnowledgeSubjectDef/KnowledgeSubjectPolicyDef/KnowledgeSharingPolicyDef/KnowledgeForgettingPolicyDef
  dependency bases are deleted. Shared declarations own JSON policy keys/directions/companies only;
  original CompanyKnowledgeService owns history, private views, exchange, forgetting and freshness.
  Contact exploration and fresh visibility resolve subject contracts, not native bases. Broader
  spatial/provider mutation and full progression gates are not inferred from the new focused case.
- All loaded IWorkEffect definitions are actual frozen immutable aliases, never definition-owned
  IPersistentService state. WorkEffectContracts owns the shared plan/preview/apply boundary;
  refusals preserve the actual target Entity.Sync, scalar/clock/identity and optional map scope.
  Native and direct independent research effects use TechnologyCompletionContracts and original
  WorkBoard retries/CompanyKnowledge. Final effect previews belong in WorkView, not host logic.
  Scope guards are not whole-world rollback or unrelated service/entity mutation proof.
- Technology unlocks are direct ITechnologyUnlock definitions with explicit current-session Matches;
  TechnologyUnlockDeclaration<T> alone authors/resolves typed target references. All loaded actual
  immutable aliases validate before Initialize, including unused declarations; no shared definition
  runtime state or obsolete base factory. Original access/learned/work/processor owners govern
  real research-to-manufacturing; utility/provider/full progression acceptance is not inferred.
- Authored plan sites explicitly declare per-candidate conditions arrays. Existing IGameCondition
  Native composite_conditions reuse original DependencyGraph and guarded leaf observation. Groups
  declare nonempty all/any IDs, no cached readiness or provider-specific scheduling. Original
  GameConditionContracts is selected-module/session scoped and owns one expiring GameConditionContext.
  Contract children and same-company view aliases reuse it; no view-local/second recursive evaluator.
  Recursive paths and swallowed errors latch original fault; expired contexts cannot invoke providers.
  This is engine-contract safety, not a sandbox for arbitrary CLR recursion or CPU.
  guard evaluates the same private view for site sources, decisions and diagnostics. Candidate
  facts expose blockers, not live condition providers. Policy-ready AND placement-ready select;
  keep blocked candidates visible with typed diagnostics. No implicit empty conditions or second
  strategy store. Site policy chooses intent; it does not revoke active/queued normal player work.
- Pending-plan recovery uses IPlanRecoveryPolicy, never a host retry. Native playbook owns current
  timestamps/attempts/tickets; normal pending-only cancellation rechecks actual state before commit.
  Exact immutable PlanCancellationSelection is the sole whole cancellation input; WorkBoard checks
  every actual selected ID before any ordinary mutation. Groups delegate to the same owner.
  Playbook cancels its entire accepted First/Count range with one normal command, not group labels
  or a per-member cursor. Preserve native links until the actual whole answer; no PendingPlan state.
  Explicit recovery:null opts out. Do not cancel/relocate/refund active or paused work automatically.
  ServiceContainer prepares immutable provider declarations once after freeze and before all validators;
  WorkBoard is the sole captured activity catalog owner. No lazy lookup/second catalog fallback.
- Simulation uses fixed point, stable IDs/order and one session-owned random source. No wall clock,
  floating-point gameplay, platform timing decisions or process-global session state.
- Exactly one game clock: GameClock alone stores current time and owns its embedded Sync.
  World constructs it once; SimGame reuses it. World.Step preflights and completes time through
  that owner after successful gameplay; World.Tick is a read-only projection, never another field.
  IGameClock is read-only for modules/entities; GameTime is immutable conversion, not a clock.
  Local elapsed work, deadlines and last-success timestamps derive from this same clock. Modules
  must never advance independent current time or run their own time/update loop.
- One `Sync(IStateSync)` owns save/load/hash for each stateful responsibility.
- Authoritative timestamps, work requirements and elapsed whole work use checked signed 64-bit ticks.
  WorkProgress owns fractional elapsed work; do not narrow progress into Fix. Per-Update work counts
  are bounded integers, not session ages. Each scenario explicitly selects an immutable authored time
  definition. Durations resolve against that session quantum; calendars are optional named projections.
  No static minute conversion, calendar default or legacy scenario time field survives.
- Hosts read public immutable views/snapshots, submit commands and drive the existing lifecycle.
  They cannot obtain mutable World state. Modules use public engine execution/composition contracts.
- Every outlet must expose the inlet contract when port capabilities are migrated.

  unchanged. See P9_EMERGENCY_PREEMPTION_DESIGN and its linked evidence.

  Existing Dirty/source/fallback triggers own pass cadence; no extra per-tick dispatcher. Original

  Aging alone does not preempt acquired jobs, revoke native/dock claims, teleport or reset age/progress.

- Do not automatically rerun test suites after each edit. Build only when compilation needs checking;
  target the specific changed behavior. Run an affected suite only when needed for the completed slice,
  not repeatedly for every change. Do not bundle broad family filters and call that focused verification.
  No full-suite or broad filtered runs unless the user explicitly asks, including at stage
  completion; keep the pending stage regression gate open until authorized and actually proven.
  Stage build gate: `dotnet build Beep.SimEngine.slnx`; oil has a separate runner.
  Counts and focused tests do not prove full regression or future stages complete.

  Recovery archives must never be compiled or packed. No Git initialization/remotes/package publishing
  without a direct request.
